  632|       |/// Create a normal CredX presentation plus a Holder-local opening for the
  633|       |/// credential-bound credit commitment.  The independent Pedersen blinder is
  634|       |/// selected before the presentation challenge and is therefore covered by the
  635|       |/// CredX signature/range transcript without being derivable from the public
  636|       |/// BBS response.
  637|       |#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
  638|     11|pub fn prove_for_committed_input(
  639|     11|    registry_b64: &str,
  640|     11|    wallet_b64: &str,
  641|     11|    task_domain: &str,
  642|     11|    binding_tag: &str,
  643|     11|    required_scope: &str,
  644|     11|    region: &str,
  645|     11|    minimum_credit: isize,
  646|     11|    global_epoch: u64,
  647|     11|    regional_epoch: u64,
  648|     11|) -> Result<CommittedInputProveResponse, ApiError> {
  649|     11|    let credit_blinder = Scalar::random(OsRng);
  650|     11|    let (proof, opening) = prove_internal(
  651|     11|        registry_b64,
  652|     11|        wallet_b64,
  653|     11|        task_domain,
  654|     11|        binding_tag,
  655|     11|        required_scope,
  656|     11|        region,

    1|       |//! S2.2 production role boundaries and public revocation-delta protocol.
    2|       |//!
    3|       |//! No function in this module accepts both issuer secret state and holder

   14|       |pub const REGIONAL_ROLE: &str = "regional-issuer";

    2|       |//!
    3|       |//! All commitments, range proofs, and replicated shares use the BLS12-381

  467|      2|pub fn prove_parent_child_commitment_equality(
  468|      2|    parent_bundle: &ConsistencyProofBundle,
  469|      2|    parent: &PrivateCommittedInputOpenings,
  470|      2|    child_bundle: &ConsistencyProofBundle,
  471|      2|    child: &PrivateCommittedInputOpenings,
  472|      2|) -> Result<ParentChildCommitmentEqualityProof, ApiError> {
  473|      2|    if parent_bundle.parameter_digest != child_bundle.parameter_digest {
  ------------------
  |  Branch (473:8): [True: 0, False: 2]
  ------------------
  |  Branch (473:8): [Folded - Ignored]
  ------------------
  474|      0|        return Err(ApiError::crypto("rebind parameter profile changed"));
  475|      2|    }
  476|      2|    let parent_digest = parent_bundle.digest()?;
                                                            ^0
  477|      2|    let child_digest = child_bundle.digest()?;
                                                          ^0
  478|      2|    let pairs = [
  479|      2|        ("credit", &parent.credit, &child.credit),
  480|      2|        ("price", &parent.price, &child.price),
  481|      2|        ("quantity", &parent.quantity, &child.quantity),
  482|      2|    ];
  483|      2|    let mut proofs = Vec::with_capacity(3);
  484|      5|    for (label, old, new) in pairs {
                                           ^2
  485|      5|        ensure_fixed_opening_generators(label, old)?;
                                                                 ^0
  486|      5|        ensure_fixed_opening_generators(label, new)?;
                                                                 ^0
  487|      5|        if old.message != new.message {
  ------------------
  |  Branch (487:12): [True: 1, False: 4]
  ------------------
  |  Branch (487:12): [Folded - Ignored]
  ------------------
  488|      1|            return Err(ApiError::crypto(
  489|      1|                "holder attempted to rebind a changed committed value",
  490|      1|            ));
  491|      4|        }
  492|      4|        let nonce = Scalar::random(OsRng);
  493|      4|        let announcement = old.blinder_generator * nonce;
  494|      4|        let challenge = equality_challenge(
  495|      4|            label,
  496|      4|            &parent_digest,
  497|      4|            &child_digest,
  498|      4|            &parent_bundle.parameter_digest,
  499|      4|            old.commitment,
  500|      4|            new.commitment,
  501|      4|            announcement,
  502|       |        );
  503|      4|        let response = nonce + challenge * (new.blinder - old.blinder);
  504|      4|        proofs.push(CommitmentEqualityProof {
  505|      4|            label: label.into(),
  506|      4|            announcement_b64: point_to_b64(announcement),
  507|      4|            response_b64: scalar_to_b64(response),
  508|      4|        });

  511|      1|        schema_version: "saib-retf-s3.0.3-parent-child-equality-proof/1".into(),
  512|      1|        parent_bundle_digest: parent_digest,
  513|      1|        child_bundle_digest: child_digest,
  514|      1|        parameter_digest: parent_bundle.parameter_digest.clone(),
  515|      1|        proofs,
  516|      1|    })
  517|      2|}

  875|    703|fn fixed_generators(label: &str) -> Result<(G1Projective, G1Projective), ApiError> {

  877|    703|        "credit" => (CREDIT_MESSAGE_DOMAIN, CREDIT_BLINDER_DOMAIN),

   83|       |        // vD(x) = ∑^{m}_{s=1}{ ∏ 1..s {yD_i + alpha}^-1 ∏ 1 ..s-1 {yD_j - x}
   84|      0|        let one = Scalar::ONE;

   76|       |
   77|       |/// The commit or blinding step for generating a ZKP
   78|       |/// The next step is to call `get_bytes_for_challenge`
   79|       |/// to create the fiat shamir heuristic
   80|       |#[derive(Debug, Copy, Clone)]
   81|       |pub struct MembershipProofCommitting {
   82|       |    e_c: G1Projective,
   83|       |    t_sigma: G1Projective,
   84|       |    t_rho: G1Projective,
   85|       |    delta_sigma: Scalar,
   86|       |    delta_rho: Scalar,
   87|       |    blinding_factor: Scalar,
   88|       |    r_sigma: Scalar,
   89|       |    r_rho: Scalar,
   90|       |    r_delta_sigma: Scalar,
   91|       |    r_delta_rho: Scalar,
   92|       |    sigma: Scalar,
   93|       |    rho: Scalar,
   94|       |    cap_r_sigma: G1Projective,
   95|       |    cap_r_rho: G1Projective,
   96|       |    cap_r_delta_sigma: G1Projective,
   97|       |    cap_r_delta_rho: G1Projective,
   98|       |    cap_r_e: Gt,
   99|       |    witness_value: Scalar,
  100|       |}
  101|       |
  102|       |impl MembershipProofCommitting {
  103|       |    /// Create a new membership proof committing phase
  104|     78|    pub fn new(
  105|     78|        y: ProofMessage<Scalar>,
  106|     78|        witness: MembershipWitness,
  107|     78|        proof_params: ProofParams,
  108|     78|        pubkey: PublicKey,
  109|     78|    ) -> Self {
  110|     78|        let message = y.get_message();
  111|     78|        let rng = rand_core::OsRng;
  112|       |        // Randomly select σ, ρ
  113|     78|        let sigma = generate_fr(SALT, None, rng);
  114|     78|        let rho = generate_fr(SALT, None, rng);
  115|       |
  116|       |        // E_C = C + (σ + ρ)Z
  117|     78|        let e_c = proof_params.z * (sigma + rho) + witness.0;
  118|       |
  119|       |        // T_σ = σX
  120|     78|        let t_sigma = proof_params.x * sigma;
  121|       |
  122|       |        // T_ρ = ρY
  123|     78|        let t_rho = proof_params.y * rho;
  124|       |
  125|       |        // δ_σ = yσ
  126|     78|        let delta_sigma = message * sigma;
  127|       |
  128|       |        // δ_ρ = yρ
  129|     78|        let delta_rho = message * rho;
  130|       |
  131|       |        // Randomly pick r_σ,r_ρ,r_δσ,r_δρ
  132|       |        // r_y is either generated randomly or supplied in case this proof is used to
  133|       |        // bind to an external proof
  134|     78|        let r_y = y.get_blinder(rng).unwrap();
  135|     78|        let r_sigma = generate_fr(SALT, None, rng);
  136|     78|        let r_rho = generate_fr(SALT, None, rng);
  137|     78|        let r_delta_sigma = generate_fr(SALT, None, rng);
  138|     78|        let r_delta_rho = generate_fr(SALT, None, rng);
  139|       |
  140|       |        // R_σ = r_σ X
  141|     78|        let cap_r_sigma = proof_params.x * r_sigma;
  142|       |
  143|       |        // R_ρ = ρY
  144|     78|        let cap_r_rho = proof_params.y * r_rho;
  145|       |
  146|       |        // R_δσ = r_y T_σ - r_δσ X
  147|     78|        let cap_r_delta_sigma = cap_r(&[t_sigma, -proof_params.x], &[r_y, r_delta_sigma]);
  148|       |
  149|       |        // R_δρ = r_y T_ρ - r_δρ Y
  150|     78|        let cap_r_delta_rho = cap_r(&[t_rho, -proof_params.y], &[r_y, r_delta_rho]);
  151|       |
  152|       |        // R_E = e(E_C, P~)^r_y
  153|     78|        let g2 = G2Projective::GENERATOR;
  154|       |
  155|       |        // R_E *= e(Z, P~)^-r_δσ - r_δρ
  156|     78|        let exp = -(r_delta_sigma + r_delta_rho);
  157|       |
  158|       |        // Optimize one less pairing by computing
  159|       |        // R_E = e(E_C^r_y + Z^{-r_δσ - r_δρ}, P~)
  160|     78|        let mut lhs = e_c * r_y;
  161|     78|        let z = proof_params.z * exp;
  162|     78|        lhs += z;
  163|     78|        let mut cap_r_e = pair(lhs, g2);
  164|       |
  165|       |        // R_E *= e(Z, Q~)^-r_σ - r_ρ
  166|     78|        let exp = -(r_sigma + r_rho);
  167|     78|        cap_r_e += pairing(proof_params.z, pubkey.0, exp);
  168|       |
  169|     78|        Self {
  170|     78|            e_c,
  171|     78|            t_sigma,
  172|     78|            t_rho,
  173|     78|            delta_sigma,
  174|     78|            delta_rho,
  175|     78|            blinding_factor: r_y,
  176|     78|            r_sigma,
  177|     78|            r_rho,
  178|     78|            r_delta_sigma,
  179|     78|            r_delta_rho,
  180|     78|            sigma,
  181|     78|            rho,
  182|     78|            cap_r_e,
  183|     78|            cap_r_sigma,
  184|     78|            cap_r_rho,
  185|     78|            cap_r_delta_sigma,
  186|     78|            cap_r_delta_rho,
  187|     78|            witness_value: message,
  188|     78|        }
  189|     78|    }

  194|     78|    pub fn get_bytes_for_challenge(&self, transcript: &mut Transcript) {

  205|       |    /// Given the challenge value, compute the s values for Fiat-Shamir and return the actual
  206|       |    /// proof to be sent to the verifier
  207|     74|    pub fn gen_proof(&self, challenge_hash: Element) -> MembershipProof {
  208|     74|        let challenge_hash = challenge_hash.0;
  209|       |        // s_y = r_y - cy
  210|     74|        let s_y = schnorr(self.blinding_factor, self.witness_value, challenge_hash);
  211|       |        // s_σ = r_σ - cσ
  212|     74|        let s_sigma = schnorr(self.r_sigma, self.sigma, challenge_hash);
  213|       |        // s_ρ = r_ρ - cρ
  214|     74|        let s_rho = schnorr(self.r_rho, self.rho, challenge_hash);
  215|       |        // s_δσ = rδσ - cδ_σ
  216|     74|        let s_delta_sigma = schnorr(self.r_delta_sigma, self.delta_sigma, challenge_hash);
  217|       |        // s_δρ = rδρ - cδ_ρ
  218|     74|        let s_delta_rho = schnorr(self.r_delta_rho, self.delta_rho, challenge_hash);
  219|     74|        MembershipProof {
  220|     74|            e_c: self.e_c,
  221|     74|            t_sigma: self.t_sigma,
  222|     74|            t_rho: self.t_rho,
  223|     74|            s_y,
  224|     74|            s_sigma,
  225|     74|            s_rho,
  226|     74|            s_delta_sigma,
  227|     74|            s_delta_rho,
  228|     74|        }
  229|     74|    }

  233|       |#[derive(Debug, Default, Copy, Clone, Deserialize, Serialize)]
  234|       |pub struct MembershipProof {
  235|       |    pub(crate) e_c: G1Projective,
  236|       |    pub(crate) t_sigma: G1Projective,
  237|       |    pub(crate) t_rho: G1Projective,
  238|       |    pub(crate) s_sigma: Scalar,
  239|       |    pub(crate) s_rho: Scalar,
  240|       |    pub(crate) s_delta_sigma: Scalar,
  241|       |    pub(crate) s_delta_rho: Scalar,
  242|       |    pub(crate) s_y: Scalar,
  243|       |}
  244|       |
  245|       |impl core::fmt::Display for MembershipProof {
  246|      0|    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut core::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> core::fmt::Result {
  247|      0|        write!(f, "MembershipProof {{ e_c: {}, t_sigma: {}, t_rho: {}, s_sigma: {}, s_rho: {}, s_delta_sigma: {}, s_delta_rho: {}, s_y: {} }}",
  248|       |        self.e_c, self.t_sigma, self.t_rho, self.s_sigma, self.s_rho, self.s_delta_sigma, self.s_delta_rho, self.s_y)
  249|      0|    }

  252|       |impl MembershipProof {
  253|       |    /// The size of the proof in bytes
  254|       |    pub const BYTES: usize = 304;
  255|       |
  256|       |    /// Generate the structure that can be used in the challenge hash
  257|       |    /// returns a struct to avoid recomputing
  258|    332|    pub fn finalize(
  259|    332|        &self,
  260|    332|        accumulator: Accumulator,
  261|    332|        proof_params: ProofParams,
  262|    332|        pubkey: PublicKey,
  263|    332|        challenge_hash: Element,
  264|    332|    ) -> MembershipProofFinal {
  265|    332|        let challenge_hash = challenge_hash.0;
  266|       |        // R_σ = s_δ X - c T_σ
  267|    332|        let cap_r_sigma = cap_r(
  268|    332|            &[proof_params.x, -self.t_sigma],
  269|    332|            &[self.s_sigma, challenge_hash],
  270|       |        );
  271|       |
  272|       |        // R_ρ = s_ρ Y - c T_ρ
  273|    332|        let cap_r_rho = cap_r(
  274|    332|            &[proof_params.y, -self.t_rho],
  275|    332|            &[self.s_rho, challenge_hash],
  276|       |        );
  277|       |
  278|       |        // R_δσ =  s_y T_σ - s_δσ X
  279|    332|        let cap_r_delta_sigma = cap_r(
  280|    332|            &[self.t_sigma, -proof_params.x],
  281|    332|            &[self.s_y, self.s_delta_sigma],
  282|       |        );
  283|       |
  284|       |        // R_δρ =  s_y T_ρ - s_δρ Y
  285|    332|        let cap_r_delta_rho = cap_r(
  286|    332|            &[self.t_rho, -proof_params.y],
  287|    332|            &[self.s_y, self.s_delta_rho],
  288|       |        );
  289|       |
  290|    332|        let g2 = G2Projective::GENERATOR;
  291|       |
  292|       |        // We can eliminate three pairings by combining
  293|       |        // e(E_C, P~)^s_y * e(Z, P~)^-(s_delta_sigma + s_delta_rho) * e(V, P~)^-c
  294|       |        // to
  295|       |        // e(E_C^s_y + Z^-(s_delta_sigma + s_delta_rho) + V^-c, P~)
  296|       |        // and
  297|       |        // e(Z, Q~)^-(s_sigma + s_rho) * e(E_C, Q~)^c
  298|       |        // to
  299|       |        // e(Z^-(s_sigma + s_rho) + E_C^c, Q~)
  300|       |
  301|       |        // e(E_C, P~)^s_y
  302|    332|        let mut lhs = self.e_c * self.s_y;
  303|       |
  304|       |        // e(Z, P~)^-(s_delta_sigma + s_delta_rho)
  305|    332|        let mut exp = -(self.s_delta_sigma + self.s_delta_rho);
  306|    332|        let mut rhs = proof_params.z * exp;
  307|    332|        lhs += rhs;
  308|       |
  309|       |        // e(V, P~)^-c
  310|    332|        exp = -challenge_hash;
  311|    332|        rhs = accumulator.0 * exp;
  312|    332|        lhs += rhs;
  313|    332|        let mut cap_r_e = pair(lhs, g2);
  314|       |
  315|       |        // e(Z, Q~)^-(s_sigma + s_rho)
  316|    332|        exp = -(self.s_sigma + self.s_rho);
  317|    332|        lhs = proof_params.z * exp;
  318|       |
  319|       |        // e(E_C, Q~)^c
  320|    332|        rhs = self.e_c * challenge_hash;
  321|    332|        lhs += rhs;
  322|    332|        cap_r_e += pair(lhs, pubkey.0);
  323|       |
  324|    332|        MembershipProofFinal {
  325|    332|            e_c: self.e_c,
  326|    332|            t_sigma: self.t_sigma,
  327|    332|            t_rho: self.t_rho,
  328|    332|            cap_r_e,
  329|    332|            cap_r_sigma,
  330|    332|            cap_r_rho,
  331|    332|            cap_r_delta_sigma,
  332|    332|            cap_r_delta_rho,
  333|    332|        }
  334|    332|    }
  335|       |
  336|       |    /// Get the byte representation of the proof
  337|      0|    pub fn to_bytes(&self) -> [u8; Self::BYTES] {
  338|      0|        let mut res = [0u8; Self::BYTES];
  339|      0|        let output = serde_bare::to_vec(self).unwrap();
  340|      0|        res.copy_from_slice(&output);
  341|      0|        res
  342|      0|    }
  343|       |
  344|       |    /// Convert a byte representation to a proof
  345|      0|    pub fn from_bytes(input: &[u8; Self::BYTES]) -> Result<Self, Error> {
  346|      0|        serde_bare::from_slice(input).map_err(|e| Error::from_msg(1, &e.to_string()))
  347|      0|    }

  565|       |
  566|       |    /// Given the challenge value, compute the s values for Fiat-Shamir and return the actual
  567|       |    /// proof to be sent to the verifier
  568|      0|    pub fn gen_proof(&self, challenge_hash: Element) -> NonMembershipProof {
  569|       |        // s_y = r_y - cy
  570|      0|        let s_y = schnorr(self.blinding_factor, self.witness_value, challenge_hash.0);
  571|       |        // s_σ = r_σ - cσ
  572|      0|        let s_sigma = schnorr(self.r_sigma, self.sigma, challenge_hash.0);
  573|       |        // s_ρ = r_ρ - cρ
  574|      0|        let s_rho = schnorr(self.r_rho, self.rho, challenge_hash.0);
  575|       |        // s_δσ = rδσ - cδ_σ
  576|      0|        let s_delta_sigma = schnorr(self.r_delta_sigma, self.delta_sigma, challenge_hash.0);
  577|       |        // s_δρ = rδρ - cδ_ρ
  578|      0|        let s_delta_rho = schnorr(self.r_delta_rho, self.delta_rho, challenge_hash.0);
  579|       |        // s_u = r_u + c d
  580|      0|        let s_u = schnorr(self.r_u, self.witness_d, challenge_hash.0);
  581|       |        // s_v = r_v + c tau
  582|      0|        let s_v = schnorr(self.r_v, self.tau, challenge_hash.0);
  583|       |        // s_w = r_w - c d pi
  584|      0|        let pi = -(self.pi * self.witness_d);
  585|      0|        let s_w = schnorr(self.r_w, pi, challenge_hash.0);
  586|       |
  587|      0|        NonMembershipProof {
  588|      0|            e_c: self.e_c,
  589|      0|            e_d: self.e_d,

  623|       |impl NonMembershipProof {
  624|       |    /// Generate the structure that can be used in the challenge hash
  625|       |    /// returns a struct to avoid recomputing
  626|      0|    pub fn finalize(
  627|      0|        &self,
  628|      0|        accumulator: Accumulator,
  629|      0|        proof_params: ProofParams,
  630|      0|        pubkey: PublicKey,
  631|      0|        challenge_hash: Element,
  632|      0|    ) -> NonMembershipProofFinal {
  633|       |        // R_σ = s_δ X - c T_σ
  634|      0|        let cap_r_sigma = cap_r(
  635|      0|            &[proof_params.x, -self.t_sigma],
  636|      0|            &[self.s_sigma, challenge_hash.0],
  637|       |        );
  638|       |
  639|       |        // R_ρ = s_ρ Y - c T_ρ
  640|      0|        let cap_r_rho = cap_r(
  641|      0|            &[proof_params.y, -self.t_rho],
  642|      0|            &[self.s_rho, challenge_hash.0],
  643|       |        );
  644|       |
  645|       |        // R_δσ =  s_y T_σ - s_δσ X
  646|      0|        let cap_r_delta_sigma = cap_r(
  647|      0|            &[self.t_sigma, -proof_params.x],
  648|      0|            &[self.s_y, self.s_delta_sigma],
  649|       |        );
  650|       |
  651|       |        // R_δρ =  s_y T_ρ - s_δρ Y
  652|      0|        let cap_r_delta_rho = cap_r(
  653|      0|            &[self.t_rho, -proof_params.y],
  654|      0|            &[self.s_y, self.s_delta_rho],
  655|       |        );
  656|       |
  657|      0|        let g2 = G2Projective::GENERATOR;
  658|       |
  659|       |        // We can eliminate multiple pairings by combining
  660|       |        // e(E_C, P~)^s_y * e(Z, P~)^-(s_delta_sigma + s_delta_rho) * e(V, P~)^-c * e(K, P~)^-s_v * e(Ed, P~)^c
  661|       |        // to
  662|       |        // e(E_C^s_y + Z^-(s_delta_sigma + s_delta_rho) + V^-c - s_v K + c E_d, P~)
  663|       |        // and
  664|       |        // e(Z, Q~)^-(s_sigma + s_rho) * e(E_C, Q~)^c
  665|       |        // to
  666|       |        // e(Z^-(s_sigma + s_rho) + E_C^c, Q~)
  667|       |
  668|       |        // e(E_C, P~)^s_y
  669|      0|        let mut lhs = self.e_c * self.s_y;
  670|       |
  671|       |        // e(Z, P~)^-(s_delta_sigma + s_delta_rho)
  672|      0|        let mut exp = -(self.s_delta_sigma + self.s_delta_rho);
  673|      0|        let mut rhs = proof_params.z * exp;
  674|      0|        lhs += rhs;
  675|       |
  676|       |        // e(V, P~)^-c
  677|      0|        exp = -challenge_hash.0;
  678|      0|        rhs = accumulator.0 * exp;
  679|      0|        lhs += rhs;
  680|       |
  681|       |        //e(K, P~)^-s_v
  682|      0|        exp = -self.s_v;
  683|      0|        rhs = proof_params.k * exp;
  684|      0|        lhs += rhs;
  685|       |
  686|       |        // e(Ed, P~)^c
  687|      0|        exp = challenge_hash.0;
  688|      0|        rhs = self.e_d * exp;
  689|      0|        lhs += rhs;
  690|      0|        let mut cap_r_e = pair(lhs, g2);
  691|       |
  692|       |        // e(Z, Q~)^-(s_sigma + s_rho)
  693|      0|        exp = -(self.s_sigma + self.s_rho);
  694|      0|        lhs = proof_params.z * exp;
  695|       |
  696|       |        // e(E_C, Q~)^c
  697|      0|        rhs = self.e_c * challenge_hash.0;
  698|      0|        lhs += rhs;
  699|      0|        cap_r_e += pair(lhs, pubkey.0);
  700|       |
  701|      0|        let g1 = G1Projective::GENERATOR;
  702|       |
  703|       |        // R_A = s_u P + s_v K - c E_d
  704|      0|        let cap_r_a = cap_r(
  705|      0|            &[g1, proof_params.k, self.e_d],
  706|      0|            &[self.s_u, self.s_v, -challenge_hash.0],
  707|       |        );
  708|       |
  709|       |        // R_B = s_w K + s_u E_d^-1 - c P
  710|      0|        let cap_r_b = cap_r(
  711|      0|            &[proof_params.k, self.e_dm1, g1],
  712|      0|            &[self.s_w, self.s_u, -challenge_hash.0],
  713|       |        );
  714|       |
  715|      0|        NonMembershipProofFinal {
  716|      0|            e_c: self.e_c,
  717|      0|            e_d: self.e_d,
  718|      0|            e_dm1: self.e_dm1,
  719|      0|            t_sigma: self.t_sigma,
  720|      0|            t_rho: self.t_rho,
  721|      0|            cap_r_a,
  722|      0|            cap_r_b,
  723|      0|            cap_r_e,
  724|      0|            cap_r_sigma,
  725|      0|            cap_r_rho,
  726|      0|            cap_r_delta_sigma,
  727|      0|            cap_r_delta_rho,
  728|      0|        }
  729|      0|    }

  429|       |
  430|       |    // dA(x) =  ∏ 1..n (yA_i - x)

  448|      0|        dd.push(td);

   91|       |    /// Assumes the proof of hidden messages
   92|       |    /// If other proofs were included, those will need to be verified another way
   93|       |    fn verify(
   94|       |        &self,
   95|       |        known_messages: &[usize],
   96|       |        sk: &Self::SecretKey,
   97|       |        nonce: Scalar,
   98|       |    ) -> CredxResult<bool> {
   99|       |        let mut known = BTreeSet::new();
  100|       |        let mut points = Vec::new();
  101|       |        for idx in known_messages {
  102|       |            if *idx >= sk.y.len() {
  103|       |                return Err(crate::error::Error::InvalidSignatureProofData);
  104|       |            }
  105|       |            known.insert(*idx);
  106|       |        }

  108|       |            if !known.contains(&i) {
  109|       |                points.push(G1Projective::GENERATOR * sk.y[i]);
  110|       |            }
  111|       |        }
  112|       |        points.push(G1Projective::GENERATOR);
  113|       |        points.push(self.commitment);
  114|       |
  115|       |        let mut scalars = self.proofs.clone();
  116|       |        scalars.push(-self.challenge);

  119|       |        let pk = PublicKey::from(sk);
  120|       |        transcript.append_message(b"public key", pk.to_bytes().as_ref());
  121|       |        let mut res = [0u8; 64];
  122|       |
  123|       |        let commitment = G1Projective::sum_of_products(points.as_ref(), scalars.as_ref());
  124|       |        transcript.append_message(
  125|       |            b"random commitment",
  126|       |            &commitment.to_affine().to_compressed(),
  127|       |        );
  128|       |        transcript.append_message(
  129|       |            b"blind commitment",
  130|       |            &self.commitment.to_affine().to_compressed(),
  131|       |        );
  132|       |        transcript.append_message(b"nonce", &nonce.to_be_bytes());
  133|       |        transcript.challenge_bytes(b"blind signature context challenge", &mut res);
  134|       |        let challenge = Scalar::from_bytes_wide(&res);
  135|       |
  136|       |        Ok(self.challenge.ct_eq(&challenge).unwrap_u8() == 1)
  137|       |    }

   74|       |    /// self.challenge == computed_challenge

   76|       |        &self,
   77|       |        public_key: &Self::PublicKey,
   78|       |        revealed_messages: &[(usize, Scalar)],
   79|       |        _challenge: Scalar,
   80|       |    ) -> CredxResult<()> {
   81|       |        // check the signature proof
   82|       |        if self
   83|       |            .sigma_1
   84|       |            .is_identity()

   86|       |            .unwrap_u8()

  107|       |            }

  114|       |        scalars.push(Scalar::ONE);
  115|       |
  116|       |        let j = G2Projective::sum_of_products(points.as_ref(), scalars.as_ref());
  117|       |
  118|       |        let res = multi_miller_loop(&[
  119|       |            (&self.sigma_1.to_affine(), &G2Prepared::from(j.to_affine())),
  120|       |            (
  121|       |                &self.sigma_2.to_affine(),

  147|       |/// Trait for abstracting a short group signature scheme
  148|       |pub trait ShortGroupSignatureScheme:
  149|       |    Sized + Clone + Debug + Serialize + for<'de> Deserialize<'de>
  150|       |{
  151|       |    /// The public key type
  152|       |    type PublicKey: PublicKey;
  153|       |    /// The secret key type
  154|       |    type SecretKey: SecretKey<PublicKey = Self::PublicKey>;
  155|       |    /// The signature type
  156|       |    type Signature: Signature<PublicKey = Self::PublicKey, SecretKey = Self::SecretKey>;
  157|       |    /// The blind signature context type
  158|       |    type BlindSignatureContext: BlindSignatureContext<SecretKey = Self::SecretKey>;
  159|       |    /// The blind signature type
  160|       |    type BlindSignature: BlindSignature<
  161|       |        PublicKey = Self::PublicKey,
  162|       |        SecretKey = Self::SecretKey,
  163|       |        Signature = Self::Signature,
  164|       |    >;
  165|       |    /// The proof of signature knowledge type
  166|       |    type ProofOfSignatureKnowledge: ProofOfSignatureKnowledge<PublicKey = Self::PublicKey>;
  167|       |    /// The proof of signature knowledge contribution type
  168|       |    type ProofOfSignatureKnowledgeContribution: ProofOfSignatureKnowledgeContribution<
  169|       |        Signature = Self::Signature,
  170|       |        PublicKey = Self::PublicKey,
  171|       |        ProofOfKnowledge = Self::ProofOfSignatureKnowledge,
  172|       |    >;
  173|       |
  174|       |    /// Create a keypair capable of signing up to `count` messages

  177|       |        rng: impl RngCore + CryptoRng,
  178|       |    ) -> CredxResult<(Self::PublicKey, Self::SecretKey)>;
  179|       |

  183|       |        M: AsRef<[Scalar]>;
  184|       |
  185|       |    /// Verify a proof of committed messages and generate a blind signature

   11|       |/// A commitment builder
   12|       |#[derive(Debug)]
   13|       |pub(crate) struct CommitmentBuilder<'a> {
   14|       |    pub(crate) commitment: G1Projective,
   15|       |    pub(crate) statement: &'a CommitmentStatement<G1Projective>,
   16|       |    /// Independent Pedersen commitment blinder retained by the holder.
   17|       |    pub(crate) commitment_blinder: Scalar,
   18|       |    /// Independent Schnorr randomizer for the commitment blinder response.
   19|       |    pub(crate) blinder_randomizer: Scalar,
   20|       |}
   21|       |
   22|       |impl<S: ShortGroupSignatureScheme> PresentationBuilder<S> for CommitmentBuilder<'_> {
   23|     74|    fn gen_proof(self, challenge: Scalar) -> PresentationProofs<S> {
   24|     74|        let blinder_proof = self.blinder_randomizer + challenge * self.commitment_blinder;
   25|     74|        CommitmentProof {
   26|     74|            id: self.statement.id.clone(),
   27|     74|            commitment: self.commitment,
   28|     74|            blinder_proof,
   29|     74|        }
   30|     74|        .into()
   31|     74|    }
   32|       |}
   33|       |
   34|       |impl<'a> CommitmentBuilder<'a> {
   35|       |    /// Creates a commitment builder
   36|     76|    pub fn commit(
   37|     76|        statement: &'a CommitmentStatement<G1Projective>,
   38|     76|        message: Scalar,
   39|     76|        message_randomizer: Scalar,
   40|     76|        commitment_blinder: Scalar,
   41|     76|        mut rng: impl RngCore + CryptoRng,
   42|     76|        transcript: &mut Transcript,
   43|     76|    ) -> CredxResult<Self> {
   44|     76|        let blinder_randomizer = Scalar::random(&mut rng);
   45|     76|        let commitment = statement.message_generator * message
   46|     76|            + statement.blinder_generator * commitment_blinder;
   47|     76|        let blind_commitment = statement.message_generator * message_randomizer
   48|     76|            + statement.blinder_generator * blinder_randomizer;
   49|       |
   50|     76|        transcript.append_message(b"", statement.id.as_bytes());
   51|     76|        transcript.append_message(
   52|     76|            b"commitment",
   53|     76|            commitment.to_affine().to_compressed().as_slice(),
   54|       |        );
   55|     76|        transcript.append_message(
   56|     76|            b"blind commitment",
   57|     76|            blind_commitment.to_affine().to_compressed().as_slice(),
   58|       |        );
   59|     76|        Ok(Self {
   60|     76|            commitment,
   61|     76|            statement,
   62|     76|            commitment_blinder,
   63|     76|            blinder_randomizer,
   64|     76|        })
   65|     76|    }

  215|       |    pub blinder_proof: Scalar,

  218|       |    /// The encrypted arbitrary data if the statement included it
  219|       |    pub ciphertext: Vec<u8>,

  221|       |
  222|       |impl VerifiableEncryptionDecryptionProof {
  223|       |    pub fn decrypt_and_verify(
  224|       |        &self,
  225|       |        decryption_key: &SecretKey<Bls12381G2Impl>,
  226|       |    ) -> CredxResult<ClaimData> {
  227|       |        // 12 for the nonce
  228|       |        // 16 for the tag
  229|       |        if self.ciphertext.len() < 12 + 16 {
  230|       |            return Err(Error::General("arbitrary data ciphertext is too short"));
  231|       |        }
  232|       |
  233|       |        let input = self.c1 * decryption_key.0;
  234|       |        let expected_commitment = self.c2 - input;
  235|       |        let nonce = Nonce::from_slice(&self.ciphertext[..12]);
  236|       |
  237|       |        let mut aes_transcript =
  238|       |            Transcript::new(b"PresentationEncryptionDecryption arbitrary data derive aes key");
  239|       |        aes_transcript.append_message(b"key ikm", input.to_compressed().as_slice());
  240|       |        let mut okm = [0u8; 32];
  241|       |        aes_transcript.challenge_bytes(b"aes key", &mut okm);
  242|       |
  243|       |        let key = aes_gcm::Key::<Aes128Gcm>::from_slice(&okm[..16]);
  244|       |        let aad = okm[16..]
  245|       |            .iter()
  246|       |            .copied()
  247|       |            .chain(self.c1.to_compressed())
  248|       |            .chain(self.c2.to_compressed())
  249|       |            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
  250|       |        let cipher = Aes128Gcm::new(key);
  251|       |        let payload = aes_gcm::aead::Payload {
  252|       |            msg: &self.ciphertext[12..],
  253|       |            aad: &aad,
  254|       |        };
  255|       |        let plaintext = cipher
  256|       |            .decrypt(nonce, payload)
  257|       |            .map_err(|_| Error::General("unable to decrypt arbitrary data: aes-128-gcm"))?;
  258|       |        let plaintext = String::from_utf8(plaintext)
  259|       |            .map_err(|_| Error::General("unable to convert decrypted data to utf-8"))?;
  260|       |        let claim = ClaimData::from_text(&plaintext)?;
  261|       |        let computed_commitment = self.message_generator * claim.to_scalar();
  262|       |        if computed_commitment != expected_commitment {
  263|       |            return Err(Error::General(
  264|       |                "Invalid decrypted data. Computed commitment does not match expected commitment.",
  265|       |            ));
  266|       |        }
  267|       |        Ok(claim)
  268|       |    }
  269|       |}

/mnt/g/paperexper/retf/saib-retf/prototype/anoncred-rust/vendor/credx-0.2.1-saib/src/statement/verifiable_encryption_decryption.rs:
    1|       |use crate::statement::Statement;
    2|       |use blsful::*;
    3|       |use elliptic_curve::group::GroupEncoding;
    4|       |use elliptic_curve::Group;
    5|       |use elliptic_curve_tools::group;
    6|       |use merlin::Transcript;
    7|       |use serde::de::DeserializeOwned;
    8|       |use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
    9|       |use uint_zigzag::Uint;
   10|       |
   11|       |/// Verifiable encryption that also allows decryption.
   12|       |///
   13|       |/// Use this statement when complete decryption is desired.
   14|       |/// The resulting proof is much larger than the one from [`VerifiableEncryptionStatement`]
   15|       |/// and slightly slower to generate.
   16|       |///
   17|       |/// The value encrypted and decrypted is a scalar value and not
   18|       |/// the original value. This is not meant to encrypt arbitrary data since
   19|       |/// arbitrary data can be of any length and cannot be proven in zero-knowledge.
   20|       |/// Instead, this is meant to encrypt a scalar value that can be proven in zero-knowledge
   21|       |/// and that's the best that can be achieved.
   22|       |///
   23|       |/// When arbitrary data is allowed,
   24|       |/// we use the proof to generate an AES key to encrypt
   25|       |/// arbitrary data then prove the encryption key is correct. But, again, this
   26|       |/// doesn't prove the encrypted data is correct. For that, verifiable decryption
   27|       |/// is needed. So the ciphertext when decrypted is proven to be correct but
   28|       |/// verifiers don't usually decrypt the data.
   29|       |#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   30|       |pub struct VerifiableEncryptionDecryptionStatement<
   31|       |    P: Group + GroupEncoding + Serialize + DeserializeOwned,
   32|       |> {
   33|       |    /// The generator for the message element
   34|       |    #[serde(with = "group")]
   35|       |    pub message_generator: P,
   36|       |    /// The encryption key for this ciphertext
   37|       |    pub encryption_key: PublicKey<Bls12381G2Impl>,
   38|       |    /// The statement id
   39|       |    pub id: String,
   40|       |    /// The other statement id
   41|       |    pub reference_id: String,
   42|       |    /// The claim index in the other statement
   43|       |    pub claim: usize,
   44|       |}
   45|       |
   46|       |impl<P: Group + GroupEncoding + DeserializeOwned + Serialize> Statement
   47|       |    for VerifiableEncryptionDecryptionStatement<P>
   48|       |{
   49|      0|    fn id(&self) -> String {
   50|      0|        self.id.clone()
   51|      0|    }
   52|       |
   53|      0|    fn reference_ids(&self) -> Vec<String> {
   54|      0|        vec![self.reference_id.clone()]
   55|      0|    }
   56|       |
   57|      0|    fn add_challenge_contribution(&self, transcript: &mut Transcript) {
   58|      0|        transcript.append_message(
   59|      0|            b"statement type",
   60|      0|            b"el-gamal verifiable encryption w/decryption",
   61|       |        );
   62|      0|        transcript.append_message(b"statement id", self.id.as_bytes());
